# S-P Promotional cards (ITCG)

1 Language
*   [中文](https://wiki.52poke.com/wiki/S-P%E5%8D%B0%E5%B0%BC%E6%96%87%E7%89%88%E7%89%B9%E5%85%B8%E5%8D%A1%EF%BC%88TCG%EF%BC%89 "S-P印尼文版特典卡（TCG） – 中文")

[← SM-P Promotional cards](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/SM-P_Promotional_cards_(ITCG) "SM-P Promotional cards (ITCG)")Asian [TCG](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Trading_Card_Game "Pokémon Trading Card Game") expansions[SV-P Promotional cards →](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/SV-P_Promotional_cards_(ITCG) "SV-P Promotional cards (ITCG)")

**S-P Promotional cards**[![Image 25](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/media/upload/thumb/d/d3/SetSymbol_SMPromo.png/30px-SetSymbol_SMPromo.png)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/File:SetSymbol_SMPromo.png)
[![Image 26](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/media/upload/e/e3/None.png)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/File:None.png)
| Cards in set | 378+ |
| --- |

| Release date |  |
| --- |

| Release period | November 2020 - February 2023 |
| --- |

The **S-P Promotional cards** are a series of [Indonesian](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_in_Indonesia "Pokémon in Indonesia") promotional cards released over the course of the **Pedang & Perisai** (English: _Sword & Shield_) Series of the [Pokémon Trading Card Game](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Trading_Card_Game "Pokémon Trading Card Game") in Indonesia, which officially began in November 2020, with the release of the [Sword & Shield](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sword_%26_Shield_(ATCG) "Sword & Shield (ATCG)") expansions. These cards feature the same "PROMO" symbol used on the corresponding [Japanese](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/S-P_Promotional_cards_(TCG) "S-P Promotional cards (TCG)"), [Korean](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/S-P_Promotional_cards_(KTCG) "S-P Promotional cards (KTCG)"), [Traditional Chinese](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/S-P_Promotional_cards_(TCTCG) "S-P Promotional cards (TCTCG)") and [Thai](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/S-P_Promotional_cards_(TTCG) "S-P Promotional cards (TTCG)") collections but feature different cards.
